# ============================================================================

# ============================================================================
# Consolidated source: common/scripted_localisation/air_cleanliness_winter_event_scripted_localisation.txt
# ============================================================================
# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - AIR WINTER EVENT SCRIPTED LOCALISATION
# ============================================================================
# Government-aware nouns used by Air Winter event prose. Formatting remains
# in ordinary localisation files so this surface contains no format tokens.
# ============================================================================

defined_text = {
	name = GetAirWinterGovernmentAuthority
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = democratic }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_authority_democratic
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = communism }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_authority_communism
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = fascism }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_authority_fascism
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = air_winter_government_authority_neutrality
	}
}

defined_text = {
	name = GetAirWinterGovernmentOfficial
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = democratic }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_official_democratic
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = communism }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_official_communism
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { has_government = fascism }
		localization_key = air_winter_government_official_fascism
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = air_winter_government_official_neutrality
	}
}

# Vanilla event pictures accept a scripted-localisation token. The exact
# Desert City receipt selects the dedicated convoy card while shared generic
# event 13 routes retain the Phase 2 image.
defined_text = {
	name = GetAirWinterEvent13Picture
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = air_winter_event_state
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				air_winter_event_desert_city_opening_receipt_is_valid = yes
			}
		}
		localization_key = GFX_report_event_air_winter_desert_water_convoy
	}
	text = {
		localization_key = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_2
	}
}
